- **Two host-wide reloads** (K3). RA(r8) and this packet's A2 each run one manager reload shared with the production units and the running market units (item E of section 13), plus one if this packet's RA is needed.
- **Length of the 503 window** (K4; Root ruling RP3; second opinion d1 P1-1). Staging serves `ba1a433` and the Main Bot Month answers 503 from the RA(r8) API start until this packet's A2 API start; the window closes only at Root's acceptance of that A2, a market stop or a failed owner step keeps it open for the owner's decision, and the window can last days (second opinion d2 P3-1). The cases that lengthen it are listed in section 4A: a stop of a job of this packet before its effect; an A2 unwind after an effect; an A2 stop after an effect that leaves the API stopped (the whole staging UI down); an A1 record that is not acceptable; an AR a01 difference of the c1 configuration or drop-in references; an owner-step failure on timing or on a failure that is not a boundary suspect; a boundary suspect at the owner step (GO-RA of this packet, then new values, pin, revision and rebuilds); an A1 failure after an effect; an RA(r8) market stop or market change; an RA(r8) end without the API proved ready (the whole staging UI down); an RA(r8) end that reopens Q-B; a usage band stall. Root keeps contingency job IDs and the owner GO path ready. Root reports to the owner when the window passes 6 hours and after any stop after an effect; the report mechanism and the normal-path estimate are in section 4A.
- **Boundary wrong** (K5). Most wrong edges halt visibly (`SCALE_DIVERGENCE` and the other codes of section 6.9). A cost clamp is visible too: it is labelled `COST_CLAMPED` (check R14) in any epoch, E0 included, and splits the cycle to null (second opinion d2 P2-4). Mitigations: the BR marker buckets per epoch, the DISAGREES check, and Root's parity rows of section 6.9 item 3c (fill, lot and marker counts against the BR result, and no `COST_CLAMPED` item in `findings.items` with `findings.capped` false, unless the BR Q5 price classes predict it); any mismatch is a boundary suspect and stops the step (second opinion d1 P1-2).
- **Compute deadline** (K6). All Time reconciliation on the shared host may approach the 10000 ms deadline and answer with a timeout. Mitigation: the A0' measurement with a BR-sized fixture (section 2.5) and the timing record of section 6.9.
- **Registry throw at import** (K7). A bad record throws when the module loads; the API then fails to start and may crash-loop. CI loads the module first; on the host, A2 readiness and the in-run unwind are the guard, and RA admits a crash-looping staging API (section 8).
- **Stale c1 process facts** (K9). A manifest that still names the c1 or c6 facts as the staging API process reference after RA(r8) fails `PRE_DRIFT_API` before any effect but spends an attempt ID. Root's manifest prep check asserts reference kind `AR_RECORD` for the A1 manifest; the tool accepts both kinds, so this check is Root's, not the tool's (second opinion d1 P2-2).
- **Re-seal order** (K10). A wrong order of the rebuild leaves a stale `sourceSha256` pin; the A2RA copy check catches it locally (section 6.1 item 3).
- **A1 admission scan and the revision 8 markers** (K11; audit d1 P1-1; Root rulings RP3 and RP4). The unchanged A1 scan would match the revision 8 A1 a01 marker and refuse `ADMISSION_SCAN` before any host contact. The minimal A1 code change of section 6.1 item 3 removes this; its tests show that a marker of another family is ignored. This packet's A1 consumed marker is `S6_STAGING_API_REPIN_2_A1_GO_CONSUMED_aNN.json`. The A2RA marker name is not family-specific, so the A2, A2M, RA and AR markers of this packet reuse the revision 8 file names; Root identifies them by folder plus body `jobFamily`, never by name, in the inventory and in every Root lookup (audit d2 P2-1). The revision 8 markers are never moved, renamed or deleted.
- **Admission-scan hazards** (K12; second opinion d1 P3-1; audit d1 P2-7). The runners scan `.qa-local` for `manifest*.json` and `GO_CONSUMED*.json` files that carry the family token, and each unlisted match refuses `ADMISSION_SCAN`. This happens before the marker is written, but it spends the 120-second GO window, inside the 503 window. Mitigations: the directory prefixes and the stray-file rule of section 10, removal of leftover dry-admission manifests, and a scan-only dry admission right before each GO, run from the real execution folder under `.qa-local`, with no `qaRoot` override (second opinion d2 P3-7). Entry count: about 44,000 entries under `.qa-local` at 2026-10-30, limit 100,000; Root recounts before every admission; test clusters must delete their data dirs (Root ruling RP5, Q4).
- **Restore source age** (K8). The P4 x4 a02 backup is from 2026-10-26; a full restore loses more writes each day. GO-A2 states its age.

## 13. Owner checklist before the effect GOs

Main packet section 12 applies; items 1, 3 and 7 are hard gates that need affirmative confirmation in GO-A2, GO-RA and GO-RA(r8). GO-A1 and GO-AR confirm items 3 and 7. Items 2, 4, 6 and 8 may be confirmed or accepted as named risks as there. Item 5 (Paper rules) stands from GO-P4.

1. **Staging alerts** (hard gate): staging TradingView alerts and webhooks may be missed during each API gap, or the owner pauses them.
3. **Who can stop staging** (hard gate): the owner names who stops staging units if needed and is reachable during each job and the browser steps.
7. **Production untouched** (hard gate): no production unit, database or configuration is in scope, and the owner knows of no planned production work during the jobs.

Additional items for this packet (letters local to this packet):

- **A. Mixed-revision pair** (hard gate, A2): the owner accepts that the staging API runs the pin P2 while the trading worker stays on `ba1a433`.
- **B. Rollback order and the RX rule** (hard gate, A2): GO-RA comes before any R5a, R4, R4H or RX; GO-RC or GO-RM may come before GO-RA, and RA gives the market units and the trading worker no command in every class; then GO-RM before R5a, R4 or R4H.
- **C. Restore source** (A2): the owner knows the full-restore source and its age, and that a full restore loses every write since it.
- **D. Browser steps** (hard gate, A2): after Root reports, the owner hard-reloads, opens the per-Bot report for Bots 2 and 3 and the Main Bot for Week, Month and All Time, and checks section 6.9 item 3 (a to f): the `qr2o10` tokens; Bot 3 reconciled and Bot 2 `EPOCH_UNKNOWN` at class pre-E0, the one Bot that the accepted BR a01 expectation names (section 2.5), under the rule of Root ruling RP1, Q-F, with condition (b) checked by Root from `failed_from` (section 6.9 item 3b); DISAGREES 0, and Root's parity rows of section 6.9 item 3c (any mismatch stops, except the marker rows of a Bot that passes condition (b), which are recorded and never stop alone); the reason note in English and Thai when a Bot fails; Main Bot Month answering again with the D behaviour; `liveEnabled` false, reported to Root, who records it in the A2 acceptance record; Root reads request timings and status codes view only; any single request over 5000 ms is a finding Root reviews before acceptance, and 10000 ms or a timeout fails the step; A2M follows. The C7 "not checked" result in English and in Thai is carried by the local rehearsal (section 4 item 8(a); audit d1 P3-7).
- **E. Reload with running market units** (named risk allowed, A2 and RA): the stream and collector timer keep running through the reload, which is host-wide and shared with the production units; a market change after the reload stops the job after readiness, or with the API stopped when it does not become ready, for the owner's decision; a market change at a later A2 check leaves the API running and stops the job for the owner's decision.
- **F. The 503 window** (known item, confirmed at GO-RA(r8) by a separate sentence in the same owner message and known again at GO-A2; stated in Root's GO-RA(r8) request; Root ruling RP6; second opinion d2 P2-2): between the RA(r8) API start and this packet's A2 API start, staging serves `ba1a433` and the Main Bot Month answers 503 again; the owner accepted the window (Shape R); the Main Bot Month serves again at this packet's A2 API start, the window closes only at Root's acceptance of that A2, and a market stop or a failed owner step keeps it open for the owner's decision; the window is about 1 to 2 hours on the normal path when the owner answers each GO promptly; longer if any GO waits, and the stop cases of section 4A can make it days (Root ruling RP8, G1); it depends on the owner's GO-AR, GO-A1 and GO-A2 (and GO-AR a02 when more than 6 hours pass between AR a01 and GO-A2) and on Root's receipt reviews; the window can last days; the cases that lengthen it are listed in section 4A and in Root's GO-RA(r8) request (a stop before an effect, an A2 unwind, an A2 stop that leaves the API stopped with the whole staging UI down, an A1 record that is not acceptable, an AR a01 configuration or drop-in difference, an owner-step failure on timing or on a failure that is not a boundary suspect, a boundary suspect at the owner step, an A1 failure after an effect, an RA(r8) market stop, an RA(r8) end without the API proved ready with the whole staging UI down, an RA(r8) end that reopens Q-B, a usage band stall); Root reports to the owner when the window passes 6 hours and after any stop after an effect.

## 14. Suggested owner GO wording

Each line ends with a `notAfter` UTC time that the owner fills in. Every line shows the pin and both revisions. Issuers (Root ruling RP1, Q-A): the owner types GO-RA(r8) and GO-A2 (with its A2M clause); GO-AR and GO-A1 are requested from the owner too, unless the owner records a standing authority for them in chat, and Root never infers one. GO-BR belongs to packet S6-QR2-BOUNDARY-READ-1. Order: GO-RA(r8), then GO-AR a01, then GO-A1, then (only under section 6.1 item 11) GO-AR a02, then GO-A2; GO-RA only if needed. Any change of the pin after a GO voids that GO. Every line whose job uses the migration connection selector or reads environment sources says so in one plain clause (ruling D4). No such GO is recorded by this preparation.

GO-RA(r8) (first; the revision 8 GO-RA template, wording unchanged, with attempt a01, the A2 a01 job ID, the pin `33e5a8c`, the production baseline and the record sources of the reviewed A2 a01 receipt filled in; the "[A2 job reference]" slot is filled with the job ID and the parenthesis "(attempt a01, A2_PASSED, 29 October 2026)", both part of the fill (Root ruling RP5, Q1, confirmed the ID against the A2 a01 root manifest; audit d2 P3-6); the production baseline is "from CHECK r6 a02", the revision 8 template option word for word, because the A2 a01 retained output's production block holds no per-unit rows (Root ruling RP5, Q3); the default line has no reset-failed clause, and Root adds the revision 8 reset-failed clause only if the RA(r8) dry admission shows a failed unit that needs it (Root ruling RP2, E6; audit d1 P3-1), while the template wording "(in RA also the reset-failed of the optional clause)" stays byte-equal (Root ruling RP5, Q2); the tool is the sealed RA(r8) of section 4A; Root's request states the 503 window of item F with the case list of section 4A, the normal-path estimate and the 6-hour report trigger, and the owner confirms item F in the same message by the separate sentence below the line):

> GO S6-STAGING-API-REPIN-1 RA attempt a01 for Staging QL-P2 Prototype only, reversing A2 job s6-a2-a01-88460b85adb72d0bdb9a5048 (attempt a01, A2_PASSED, 29 October 2026): the staging API returns from API pin 33e5a8c3c31b0fca61fa44792aad7d1cea769c35 (includes the QR-2 422 range refusal and panel hint: yes; includes the QR-2 bar-coverage degrade D: yes) to revision ba1a433 (includes QR-2 O7: yes) with the QR-2 flag still effective 1, and the trading worker stays on revision ba1a433 and keeps running: in one SSH session with the reviewed tool, first re-check, read only, the host health (another S6 job in flight, an unreadable process table or an unexpected Node major refuses), the systemd version, that no systemd job is pending other than the staging API's own start, stop or restart job and that no unit needs a reload other than the staging API in a recognised layer state below whose pending reload the reviewed A2 or prior RA receipt, or, when that receipt was lost, its host progress record, shows (a rename or archive of the live re-pin layer, labelled attempted or done with the file on disk in the matching form and digest, without a reload labelled done after it), the login manager's setting for killing user processes at logout (and stop if that setting is on or unknown), the four production units against the production baseline from CHECK r6 a02 (a difference ends the job before any effect as `RA_PRODUCTION_DIFFERENCE`), the staging API's drop-in layers, environment sources (the same keys as A2, without the database connection key) and the database read only through the migration connection selector of the B2 Window 2 precedent against the reviewed A2 receipt, the staging API and trading-worker journals (they must be readable, and the 60-second read must not be filled to the line cap; the read since A2 is recorded only; counts recorded, never a stop), and the research worker as stopped; the market stream, the fallback collector service, the fallback collector timer and the trading worker are each classified, with the environment properties left out of every unit property read other than the host-side environment-source read of the staging API's own environment property (digest only), as running with any process, inactive or failed, with the class, process continuity and restart count recorded and never compared with the c6, A1, AR or A2 values (a unit in automatic restart gets a read-only settle wait of up to 30 seconds; an unknown state, a state still transitioning after that wait, or a pending job refuses); the trading worker's heartbeat rows and freshness are recorded, and the gate refuses only on an unreadable queue (also a row without a string role), a Live-mode row, a Live signal above zero, an empty set of rows of the trading worker role while the trading worker runs, more than one fresh row whose tick is at or after the trading worker's main-process start minus one second, or any other fresh row that is not a previous process's row (with a running trading worker, a row with a tick before its main-process start; with no running trading worker, a row whose tick does not advance across two reads at least twice the heartbeat interval plus 250 ms apart; such a row is recorded, not refused), never on a stale heartbeat in any class (a named relaxation of the R5a stale-heartbeat refusal, because the tool gives the trading worker no command); all of this under the two-way hooks-and-links rule of A2, and, only when the timer is active, the timer window (a read-only wait of at most 420 seconds, otherwise a stop before any effect); a collector run in progress is waited out, read only, within the same 420-second bound, otherwise a stop before any effect; the environment sources are read only inside the host-side process, and the tool returns booleans, counts and digests only and is not a binding read. This GO is admissible after GO-RC or GO-RM, and the tool gives the market units and the trading worker no command in every class. The staging API may be running, restarting (also in automatic restart, in the activating state with a start sub-state, or with its own pending start, stop or restart job, after the settle wait, for example a crash loop), stopped or failed (another state still transitioning after that wait refuses): the tool stops it if it is running or restarting and proves it stopped (if that stop is not proved within 100 seconds or the API becomes failed, the tool makes no further control call and reports; an error in the proof wait counts as not proved and sends no further command, a command timeout is a stop not proved, and any other failure of the stop command gets one snapshot read, so that the tool ends unwound with nothing started only when the snapshot shows the API running with no job and the gate's main process, or failed with no job), re-checks that no collector run and no job is pending (a read-only wait of up to 15 seconds for a run to end), and acts by the layer state it finds on disk, which must be one of the packet's recognised states, with the drop-in files on disk equal to that state (the live drop-in list and the pending and archive forms of the re-pin layer's name; every other file of the drop-in directory equal in count and digest to the A2 gate inventory, from the reviewed A2 receipt or, when that receipt was lost, from the gate line of the A2 host progress record; with no such line A2 had no effect, and the tool refuses; an archive of the layer's name that no listed A2 or prior RA record explains refuses): with the re-pin layer live, it archives the layer by no-clobber rename; with only a pending re-pin file and no listed record of its rename (a rename counts as shown only when labelled done, or labelled attempted with the file renamed on disk), it records that file's digest in its progress record and then archives the file by no-clobber rename, deleting nothing; with the layer or a pending file archived by the A2 unwind as the A2 receipt or progress record shows, with the layer archived by an earlier RA attempt, with a pending file archived by an earlier RA attempt whose digest matches the line recorded before its rename, or never written (only when no listed A2 or RA record shows a write, rename or archive line of the re-pin layer, labelled attempted or done), it changes no file; it runs one manager reload (host-wide, shared with the production units) if it archived the live re-pin layer or the staging API needs a reload, then checks that the loaded configuration shows the post-P5a drop-in list, the ba1a433 working directory and start command and the QR-2 flag effective 1 from the P5a flag layer, and that the production identity is unchanged, re-checks the market units against its own gate reading, and starts the API. Before every rename, archive, put-back and reload it writes an attempted line with the expected file form and digest into its progress record, and a done line after it; its receipt carries the pre-rename digest line of a pending file. If a step fails before the API is ready, the tool unwinds as section 8 of the packet states: when it archived the layer itself and the archive, the manager reload, the loaded-configuration check or the production-identity check fails, it puts the A2 layer back by rename, runs one more reload (so at most two manager reloads in this job, both host-wide and shared with the production units and the running market units), checks the A2 configuration and the staged tree (a mismatch or an unreadable tree sends no start) and starts the API on it only if the API was running when the job began; when the API start or readiness fails after it archived the layer itself and the API was running when the job began, it stops the API, puts the layer back, reloads, checks the staged tree as in the case above (a mismatch or an unreadable tree sends no start) and starts the API on the A2 configuration (if the API becomes failed during that stop, the put-back and its reload still run, nothing is stopped or started, and the failed API is recorded); if it stops before changing the layer, it starts the API again on the configuration it found (after a check of the tree that start uses; a mismatch or an unreadable tree sends no start), only if the API was running when the job began, and, when that is the post-P5a configuration with no live or pending re-pin file and no pending reload, runs the immediate checks and ends `RA_API_READY`, `RA_STOP_AT_CHECK` or `RA_PRODUCTION_DIFFERENCE`, never `RA_STOP_MARKET_AFTER_READY`. In these named cases it leaves the API stopped and Root reports: when the put-back reload, the A2 configuration check after it, or the start after the put-back or the restart on the configuration it found fails; in every other case of a failed start or readiness (with the re-pin layer archived or never written); when the re-pin layer was already archived or never written and the reconciling reload (also a time-budget stop or a failed progress write at it), the loaded-configuration check or the production-identity check fails, or its archive of a pending file is on disk and that step then fails; when it cannot put an archived layer back; when a stop is not proved or the API becomes failed; and on a later time-budget stop or failed progress write other than at that reconciling reload (after its own archive of the live layer it first puts the layer back and reloads, with no start). After its start command and before readiness is proved, a step-budget or in-run-limit stop first gets one proved stop, the put-back in that row runs only after a proved stop, no start follows, and a stop not proved ends the job as a stop not proved (ruling R12 item 6; ruling R13 item 9). No end code claims that the API stopped without a proved stop: while readiness of that start is not proved (the readiness poll and any check before readiness of that start is proved), a throw after any start command that is not followed by a proved stop ends the job as a stop not proved; after readiness is proved the existing check codes stand. After the in-run limit or, while unwinding, the unwind limit, one read-only class read records the API state, or unknown (limit). If the production identity differs at any step after the gate, the tool starts nothing from that point (after its own archive of the live layer it still puts the layer back and reloads). If the market re-check after the file action (and the reload, if one ran) finds a market unit changed from its own gate reading, the tool does not unwind and runs no extra reload: it gives the market units no command, starts the API on ba1a433, runs the immediate checks and stops, and I decide on GO-RC or GO-RM; if the API start or readiness then fails, it stops the API if it runs and proves the stop, starts nothing (it sends no stop to a failed API) and leaves the API stopped on the post-P5a configuration, and I decide. If an immediate check fails after the API is ready, the tool gives no command, runs no unwind and no reload, leaves the API running on ba1a433 and stops for review by Root. The same holds for a throw, a step-budget stop or an in-run-limit stop at an immediate check, and for a market change seen with a failed immediate check (the end stays the check stop, with the market change recorded); after the API is ready, or after a market change, the tool sends no command at all other than the start, readiness poll and stop of the market branch above (in RA also the reset-failed of the optional clause). A graceful termination never sends a command in any phase (no stop, no put-back, no start); before the first effect (the first host-changing step of the mode: a control command, a rename or archive, a pending-file write or a manager reload) it ends PRE_TERMINATED with no effect, and after the first effect it ends EFF_TERMINATED, with the end row from the disk and the API state as read; the RA family treats EFF_TERMINATED as a termination with no end code and never as the API ready, and the result says terminated. RA outcomes: `RA_API_READY`, `RA_STOP_MARKET_AFTER_READY` and `RA_STOP_AT_CHECK` end with the API ready on `ba1a433`; `RA_UNWOUND`, `RA_UNWIND_RELOAD_FAILED`, `RA_UNWIND_START_FAILED`, `RA_API_NOT_READY`, `RA_API_STOPPED`, `RA_BUDGET_STOP`, `RA_STOP_NOT_PROVED` and `RA_STOP_MARKET_API_NOT_READY` end in a recognised layer state without the API proved ready on `ba1a433` by RA's immediate checks; `RA_UNWIND_FAILED` and `RA_PRODUCTION_DIFFERENCE` are excluded. After an RA attempt that did not end with the API ready on `ba1a433`, also after a termination, which never counts as an end with the API ready and is followed by the lost-receipt wait of 1335 seconds, Root may request the next RA attempt under a new GO-RA that lists the prior attempts; the tool admits it only when the files on disk form a recognised layer state, the prior records show the facts that state needs, and production equals the baseline. Excluded: after `RA_UNWIND_FAILED` (an archived layer could not be put back) Root rules by a packet revision before any next RA; after `RA_PRODUCTION_DIFFERENCE` (a production-identity difference read at any RA step, at the gate before any effect, the RD-32 state) no next RA is admitted without Root's recorded review of the difference in its manifest, which also sets the production baseline that RA compares against. An RA that ends with the API ready on `ba1a433`, other than with `RA_PRODUCTION_DIFFERENCE`, is terminal for the RA family, and no next RA follows it; its receipt is the accepted RA receipt, for the RX tripwire and the return of R5a, R4, R4H, RM and RC, when its end row is L0-n, LU-n, LR-n or LQ-n, the post-P5a configuration is loaded, production is unchanged and the API is ready, after Root accepts it, with a Root review first for a market or check stop; a `RA_PRODUCTION_DIFFERENCE` receipt with the API ready on `ba1a433` in such an end row may be accepted by Root in the same way after its review of the difference. A Root-reviewed A2 receipt with end row L0-n or LU-n, no pending file, the API ready on `ba1a433`, the post-P5a configuration loaded and production unchanged reopens that chain in the same way, with no RA. I know that a running API can be left stopped, and that Root then reports and I decide. Immediate checks against the ba1a433 served manifest (health 200 in Paper mode, or a 503 with Paper-only mode only when the trading worker shows the same not-running class (inactive or failed) at the gate read and at the immediate read); no other fresh heartbeat row (under the tick-time rule above) and no Live-mode row in every class; the trading worker's own heartbeat (exactly one own row) is checked only when it was fresh at the gate and the trading worker is still the same process, and recorded otherwise. if the API is failed, clear it with reset-failed right before this start only, never before an unwind start (this optional clause sets the manifest boolean resetFailedGranted to true; without it a failed API refuses before any effect)]. Before every rename, archive, put-back and reload it writes an attempted line with the expected file form and digest into its progress record, and a done line after it; its receipt carries the pre-rename digest line of a pending file. If a step fails before the API is ready, the tool unwinds as section 8 of the packet states: when it archived the layer itself and the archive, the manager reload, the loaded-configuration check or the production-identity check fails, it puts the A2 layer back by rename, runs one more reload (so at most two manager reloads in this job, both host-wide and shared with the production units and the running market units), checks the A2 configuration and the staged tree (a mismatch or an unreadable tree sends no start) and starts the API on it only if the API was running when the job began; when the API start or readiness fails after it archived the layer itself and the API was running when the job began, it stops the API, puts the layer back, reloads, checks the staged tree as in the case above (a mismatch or an unreadable tree sends no start) and starts the API on the A2 configuration (if the API becomes failed during that stop, the put-back and its reload still run, nothing is stopped or started, and the failed API is recorded); if it stops before changing the layer, it starts the API again on the configuration it found (after a check of the tree that start uses; a mismatch or an unreadable tree sends no start), only if the API was running when the job began, and, when that is the post-P5a configuration with no live or pending re-pin file and no pending reload, runs the immediate checks and ends `RA_API_READY`, `RA_STOP_AT_CHECK` or `RA_PRODUCTION_DIFFERENCE`, never `RA_STOP_MARKET_AFTER_READY`. In these named cases it leaves the API stopped and Root reports: when the put-back reload, the A2 configuration check after it, or the start after the put-back or the restart on the configuration it found fails; in every other case of a failed start or readiness (with the re-pin layer archived or never written); when the re-pin layer was already archived or never written and the reconciling reload (also a time-budget stop or a failed progress write at it), the loaded-configuration check or the production-identity check fails, or its archive of a pending file is on disk and that step then fails; when it cannot put an archived layer back; when a stop is not proved or the API becomes failed; and on a later time-budget stop or failed progress write other than at that reconciling reload (after its own archive of the live layer it first puts the layer back and reloads, with no start). After its start command and before readiness is proved, a step-budget or in-run-limit stop first gets one proved stop, the put-back in that row runs only after a proved stop, no start follows, and a stop not proved ends the job as a stop not proved (ruling R12 item 6; ruling R13 item 9). No end code claims that the API stopped without a proved stop: while readiness of that start is not proved (the readiness poll and any check before readiness of that start is proved), a throw after any start command that is not followed by a proved stop ends the job as a stop not proved; after readiness is proved the existing check codes stand. After the in-run limit or, while unwinding, the unwind limit, one read-only class read records the API state, or unknown (limit). If the production identity differs at any step after the gate, the tool starts nothing from that point (after its own archive of the live layer it still puts the layer back and reloads). If the market re-check after the file action (and the reload, if one ran) finds a market unit changed from its own gate reading, the tool does not unwind and runs no extra reload: it gives the market units no command, starts the API on ba1a433, runs the immediate checks and stops, and I decide on GO-RC or GO-RM; if the API start or readiness then fails, it stops the API if it runs and proves the stop, starts nothing (it sends no stop to a failed API) and leaves the API stopped on the post-P5a configuration, and I decide. If an immediate check fails after the API is ready, the tool gives no command, runs no unwind and no reload, leaves the API running on ba1a433 and stops for review by Root. The same holds for a throw, a step-budget stop or an in-run-limit stop at an immediate check, and for a market change seen with a failed immediate check (the end stays the check stop, with the market change recorded); after the API is ready, or after a market change, the tool sends no command at all other than the start, readiness poll and stop of the market branch above (in RA also the reset-failed of the optional clause). A graceful termination never sends a command in any phase (no stop, no put-back, no start); before the first effect (the first host-changing step of the mode: a control command, a rename or archive, a pending-file write or a manager reload) it ends PRE_TERMINATED with no effect, and after the first effect it ends EFF_TERMINATED, with the end row from the disk and the API state as read; the RA family treats EFF_TERMINATED as a termination with no end code and never as the API ready, and the result says terminated. RA outcomes: `RA_API_READY`, `RA_STOP_MARKET_AFTER_READY` and `RA_STOP_AT_CHECK` end with the API ready on `ba1a433`; `RA_UNWOUND`, `RA_UNWIND_RELOAD_FAILED`, `RA_UNWIND_START_FAILED`, `RA_API_NOT_READY`, `RA_API_STOPPED`, `RA_BUDGET_STOP`, `RA_STOP_NOT_PROVED` and `RA_STOP_MARKET_API_NOT_READY` end in a recognised layer state without the API proved ready on `ba1a433` by RA's immediate checks; `RA_UNWIND_FAILED` and `RA_PRODUCTION_DIFFERENCE` are excluded. After an RA attempt that did not end with the API ready on `ba1a433`, also after a termination, which never counts as an end with the API ready and is followed by the lost-receipt wait of 1335 seconds, Root may request the next RA attempt under a new GO-RA that lists the prior attempts; the tool admits it only when the files on disk form a recognised layer state, the prior records show the facts that state needs, and production equals the baseline. Excluded: after `RA_UNWIND_FAILED` (an archived layer could not be put back) Root rules by a packet revision before any next RA; after `RA_PRODUCTION_DIFFERENCE` (a production-identity difference read at any RA step, at the gate before any effect, the RD-32 state) no next RA is admitted without Root's recorded review of the difference in its manifest, which also sets the production baseline that RA compares against. An RA that ends with the API ready on `ba1a433`, other than with `RA_PRODUCTION_DIFFERENCE`, is terminal for the RA family, and no next RA follows it; its receipt is the accepted RA receipt, for the RX tripwire and the return of R5a, R4, R4H, RM and RC, when its end row is L0-n, LU-n, LR-n or LQ-n, the post-P5a configuration is loaded, production is unchanged and the API is ready, after Root accepts it, with a Root review first for a market or check stop; a `RA_PRODUCTION_DIFFERENCE` receipt with the API ready on `ba1a433` in such an end row may be accepted by Root in the same way after its review of the difference. A Root-reviewed A2 receipt with end row L0-n or LU-n, no pending file, the API ready on `ba1a433`, the post-P5a configuration loaded and production unchanged reopens that chain in the same way, with no RA. I know that a running API can be left stopped, and that Root then reports and I decide. Immediate checks against the ba1a433 served manifest (health 200 in Paper mode, or a 503 with Paper-only mode only when the trading worker shows the same not-running class (inactive or failed) at the gate read and at the immediate read); no other fresh heartbeat row (under the tick-time rule above) and no Live-mode row in every class; the trading worker's own heartbeat (exactly one own row) is checked only when it was fresh at the gate and the trading worker is still the same process, and recorded otherwise. The market stream and the fallback collector service and timer get no command. QR-2 audit rows from panel use stay. The tool writes one record directory with a progress record and a result file in the dedicated protected backup directory, and the shell's temporary here-document file that carries the tool. I know that staging alerts and webhooks are not served during the API gap of up to 380 seconds, expected about 4 to 13 seconds, and that the stream and collector timer may keep running through the reload. I confirm checklist items 1, 3 and 7, and I accept items [E and list] as named risks. No retry. notAfter [UTC].

not recorded on `A2_PASSED` or `RA_API_READY`) and for `marketStopProved` (written only in the A2 market branch and only when the branch sent its stop: true, false, or absent), the failed-API road (`stopProved` true, `apiFailed` true), the RA case 5 failed stop command with a stopped read (`RA_STOP_NOT_PROVED`, `stopProved` false), and a failed API with a queued start job (not stopped: the stop poll continues and the end is not proved; fix round 6, G1) (ruling R25 items 2 and 7; ruling R24 item 4, F5); the real-path rule (a linked parent passes; a linked root and a non-canonical bound root refuse); the served-URL rule (a served list with `?v=` tokens passes in A2 and in RA, and in the rehearsal; a query other than `v=<token>`, an absolute URL, a `..` segment, a missing leading `/`, a leading `//` and a path of more than 1024 bytes each refuse; ruling R24 item 3, reading 6); no `argvMax` key in the A2 manifest and the roles-hash check in every mode (ruling R19 item 4(l)); and, for fix rounds 5 and 6 (rulings R24 and R25): every budget-stop code with the `PRE_` and the `EFF_` prefix in RA rows L0-n and LU-n, routed alike at the same step, with case 5 answering first (`RA_UNWOUND`, no command, in the PRECHECK and FILE phases before the rename) and `RA_BUDGET_STOP` after the port check (F1, as built; ruling R25 item 3), and a budget stop in START with the effect flag false and the API read running, which sends zero commands (G2); in A2 with the effect flag false, a clock refusal of the first stop and a budget stop after the record directory and before the stop, each with zero commands and the code `PRE_TIME_BUDGET`, and the stop sent and then failed, which takes the unwind (F2); a prior RA record ending `PRE_TERMINATED` and one ending `EFF_TERMINATED`, each refused before 1335 seconds and admitted after, and a terminated record with no start time, refused (F3); the case 5 restart refused for `TREE` or `TREE_UNKNOWN` with no start open, ending `RA_UNWIND_START_FAILED` with `startRefused` and `startRefusedAt` `CASE5`, no stop-if-runs check and no command after the refusal, with the row's disk reads and the one capped end class read still run, also when that read fails (F4, as built; ruling R25 item 4); plus mutation probes and every stop path; the build asserts the inequalities of section 11.1; architecture auditor (Opus 5.5 / high); second opinion (Opus 5.5 / high as the recorded Fable substitute; Fable is paused). The nine adapters are built in their own lane (ADP), in its own directory, and are reviewed inside the A2RA fix rounds (ruling R17 item 2), where the lane keeps its own suite and mutants; they are integrated into the generator only after the build fix round and ADP are both accepted; no mode, A2M and AR included, is accepted for a real run before then (ruling R9 item 11e).
- **Local rehearsal** before GO-RA(r8): section 4 item 8 (the revision 8 items, the reconciliation note in English and Thai, the BR-sized measurement), D34 form, items not shown locally named.
- **Rebuild** (change map section 2, as amended by Root rulings RP3 and RP4; audit d2 P1-1, P3-7). ADP and A2RA: a new snapshot each, regenerated by its own generator, constants only. A1: a copy by value of the accepted family build `s6-staging-api-repin-build-a1-2026-10-30-repin2-family` (`toolSourceSha` `ed0794a3...`) (Root rulings RP4 and RP7) into a new folder `s6-staging-api-repin-build-a1-2026-10-30-repin2`, constants only; its `gen/regen.sh` is never run. The A1 marker-family change is built and accepted (RP4) and is not built again. Re-sealed in the order ADP, A1, A2RA. Checks after the A1 rebuild: `FAMILY_TOKEN` present in `a1-core.mjs` and `a1-runner.mjs`; the family suite 13 of 13 plus the RP4 P3-1 test (a REPIN-1-named file whose body names the own `JOB_FAMILY` still blocks a01) and the RP7 test (the unanchored regex case); the full A1 suite green; `toolSourceSha` re-derived; `mutants.mjs` rerun with the four RP3 mutants added and sealed; and an independent A1 tester run (suites and mutant runner) on a quiet machine. Suites, mutants and rehearsal rerun for every tool; tester (Sonnet 5.5 / medium) and architecture auditor (Opus 5.5 / high), with the Opus 5.5 / high second opinion for the GO packet. One added A1 fixture: a drop-in directory that holds an archive of another pin's new-form layer (boolean true; archived count plus one).
- Fix rounds re-verify only the changed files with one rerun of the suite. Mutation runs and test-running reviewers are serialized. No reviewer grants acceptance; Root alone accepts each tool before admission and each result afterwards.

## 16. Explicit exclusions

This packet excludes:
- any change to a QR-2 budget value (option A waits for a later pin);
- any D code beyond the reviewed D commit that the pin carries;
- any change to the trading worker: no tree, layer, stop, start or restart;
- any start, stop, restart or reset-failed of the market stream, the fallback collector service, the fallback collector timer or the research worker;
- any daemon reload other than the one reload of A2 and of RA and, in an unwind, at most one more; any control command or reload by A1, A2M or AR;
- a database dump, a counter baseline, a session wait, a migration or any database write by the tools;
- any binding read or binding update; any unit-file, boot-setting, environment-file or linger change;
- any change to the P1, P4, P5a, P4M, collector or diagnosis tools, or to the main, follow-up or collector packets;
- an online backup refresh, a restore, or any delete of a tree, layer, pending file, backup or database (RA archives a pending file by rename);
- any binding read on the host (the trading-worker environment and stream configuration references are copied into the private manifests at build time);
- Pine Bridge activation, research admission, Preflight, profile enrollment, set B, P5b;
- Live trading, a guard reset, Best Inputs apply, the optimizer, P13j or N1 steps;
- any production change;
- any log or journal content beyond counts of fixed event kinds and refusal codes;
- any retry under the same GO;
- any edit of the revision 8 packet file (its bytes stay the RA(r8) contract) and any change to the sealed RA(r8);
- any tool code change beyond the constants of section 6.1 item 3 and the one minimal A1 marker-family change named there (Root ruling RP3, built and accepted by Root ruling RP4);
- a values commit that touches `public/`, `src/config.js`, `src/postgres/server.js` or an environment read;
- the boundary read itself (packet S6-QR2-BOUNDARY-READ-1);
- any return from state S-0 to the revision 8 state S-r8 (Shape R does not go back).

Spot and Paper only; BINANCE:BTCUSDT Spot 1m. Private acceptance NOT RUN; READY false; P13j and N1 held.
